## Edge legend

| Style | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `-->` solid | explicit `[[wikilink]]` or markdown link |

Soft edges (shared tags, co-headings) are omitted here to keep the diagram legible — they live in `notegraph.json` and the HTML view.
